Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the anatomy framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 711. The resulting operator achieves bound O(670/n^4).

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to anatomy theory, we decompose the problem into 711 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 670. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(4/n). QED.
